# Skills · S · part 108

839 entries, single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-annotation to sistema-de-execucao-sem-perfeccionismo. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-annotation**: Use when a published 10x Chromium MEF-to-iEP reprogramming scRNA-seq count matrix must be reprocessed into PAGA clusters with marker-based annotation of fibroblast, transition, dead-end, and reprogrammed iEP states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-annotation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when you must quantitatively compare scRNA-seq clustering methods against ground-truth cell-type annotations on datasets with 15-30k cells, using ARI/AMI/NMI and number-of-cluster accuracy metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-stability-analysis**: Use when you have raw vs. imputed scRNA-seq expression matrices with known cell-type labels and must quantify whether imputation improves or distorts cell clustering quality and gene-level dispersion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-clustering-stability-analysis))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-confounder-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq expression matrices contain unmeasured confounders (e.g. cell cycle, batch) and a set of annotated control genes is available; benchmark against PCA, LMM, RUV, scLVM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-confounder-inference))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-consensus-clustering**: Use when a scRNAseq (or spatial transcriptomics) dataset in SingleCellExperiment form needs a robust cell-type clustering that outperforms any single algorithm, by ensembling seven base clusterers into one consensus via binary similarity matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-consensus-clustering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-data-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic scRNA-Seq datasets with known cell-type labels to benchmark clustering or transfer-learning methods, especially across controlled source-target cell-type overlap scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-emulation**: Use when simulated molecular abundances must be transformed into a count matrix whose library sizes, fraction of zeros, and mean-variance behaviour match a real single-cell omics reference dataset for benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-emulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-imputation**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI counts (e.g., UC mucosa cells) need denoised/imputed expression values via the scPhere decoder, and when dropouts must be reduced without introducing false-positive expression of marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-imputation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-loading**: Use when preparing scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. .h5ad, PBMC interferon-beta or TICA subsets) for GeneVector, which requires raw (unnormalized) counts in AnnData .X before discretize_genes() and GeneVectorDataset construction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-loading))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-loading**: Use when starting an scRNAseq analysis from raw benchmark count data (Zhengmix, SCMixology) and a SingleCellExperiment object with a standardized feature set is needed before dimension reduction with corral or glmPCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-loading))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-processing**: Use when starting from a raw scRNA-seq read count matrix (Rds/CSV of genes x cells, e.g., Fluidigm C1 alpha cells) that must be gene-filtered, covariate-annotated, and formatted before surrogate-variable or marker-gene analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-processing))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-quality-comparison**: Use when a simulated scRNA-seq count matrix must be shown to have data characteristics (library size, zero fraction, dispersion, correlations) matching a real reference count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-quality-comparison))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., data1-data5 scenarios with defined zero-inflation levels) to benchmark dimensionality-reduction or clustering methods before running them on real data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-matrix-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-modeling**: Use when you have a raw single-cell count matrix (cells nested within individuals) and need gene-level covariate association inference that controls for drop-out (zero inflation), overdispersion, and within-sample correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-modeling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-normalization**: Use when a sparsely-connected autoencoder (SCA) or similar cluster-level analysis must be run on a raw UMI counts matrix and you need to test whether input normalization changes cluster quality metrics or near-threshold cluster calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., Mouse Cell Atlas bone marrow) must be converted into SINGE-compatible .mat files with expression matrix X, gene_list, pseudotimes, and an optional TF-only regix before GLG network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-preprocessing--ddc66a19ad110901))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices (e.g., AnnData .h5ad from a multi-batch atlas like the NEURIPS bone marrow challenge) must be cleaned, normalized to 10000 counts per cell, log1p-transformed, and reduced to highly variable genes before topic-model or VAE training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-preprocessing--247ba377897d021c))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation**: Use when you need controlled scRNA-seq test data with known per-cell circadian phases to benchmark phase-inference algorithms or study how library size and sampling design affect phase-estimation error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation--1d8e032d9dfc44e4))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation**: Use when you need controlled synthetic two-batch scRNA-seq benchmarks with known batch-effect magnitude, dropout rates, batch sizes, or cell-type composition for evaluating integration or batch-correction methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation--b528e7cbc678600d))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation**: Use when you need gold-standard scRNA-seq count matrices with known population structure, DE genes, or parameter ground truth to benchmark clustering/DE methods or to plan experiments for rare-population detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation--9eba51c0826d74b4))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation**: Use when you need a synthetic sparse count matrix with a known perturbation (e.g., swapped genes) to validate whether a comparative GRN workflow detects true signal without spurious findings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-count-simulation--7da0981e99188124))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-covariate-modeling**: Use when an (HTOs x droplets) count matrix and the total number of genes detected per droplet are both available, and more accurate probabilistic droplet assignment is needed than a naive HTO-only model provides. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-covariate-modeling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-harmonization**: Use when cell-type labels must be compared across two or more scRNA-seq studies with matching labels (e.g., excitatory vs inhibitory neurons), and you need quantitative AUROC evidence of cross-study replicability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-harmonization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation**: Use when a downstream method (clustering, trajectory inference, DE analysis) needs synthetic scRNA-seq/SRT data with ground truth for groups, batches, DE genes, or differentiation trajectories, and method choice must be justified by benchmark evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation**: Use when you must benchmark cross-timepoint cell annotation methods under controlled scenarios (shifted compositions, disappearing or emerging cell types) with ground-truth labels and Monte Carlo replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation--71a1ad17a4c7f9ac))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq dimensionality reduction or clustering tools requires ground-truth labels plus controlled sparsity and CV of TGC, e.g. testing robustness to &gt;90% zeros and dropout-driven variability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation--8d695e7721fe6009))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation**: Use when evaluating a cluster-purity metric (e.g., ROGUE) against sequencing-depth differences or cell-number variation, by generating simulated replicate scRNA-seq mixtures with known ground-truth purity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation--518679ea6921e6ad))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking spatial subpopulation or spatial-domain methods against a known truth: simulated paired scRNA-seq/ST data with ground-truth subcluster labels are needed for ARI-style evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-simulation--f57cd89b2dcbe6d2))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-subsampling**: Use when benchmarking scalability of a single-cell method: you have a cell x gene expression matrix (e.g., PBMC Multiome from the Human Blood Atlas) and need runtime vs. cell-number measurements on nested subsamples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-subsampling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-data-subsetting**: Use when building synthetic rare-cell detection benchmarks by subsampling known cell types from a large scRNA-seq expression matrix, or when probing how few DE genes preserve rare-cell identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-data-subsetting))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-integration**: Use when two raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. 10X PBMC 8k and Pan T cells) need batch integration in gene space, with quality judged by mean iLISI and mean 50-RNN metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-integration))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-mapping**: Use when two library-depth-normalized scRNA-seq expression matrices (e.g. TPM/CPM gene-by-cell data frames) exist, one with known reference clusters, and target cells must be assigned to equivalent reference cell types, especially with batch effect or imbalanced cell numbers/library sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-mapping))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-resampling**: Use when benchmarking the scalability of simulation methods: you need datasets spanning a controlled size grid (e.g., 40 sizes from 100–10,000 cells and 500–10,000 genes) to record wall-clock time and peak memory per run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-resampling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-splitting**: Use when training a FAVA VAE (or similar latent-space model) on single-cell RNA-seq data and you need to demonstrate the model generalizes to unseen cells rather than memorizing the training set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-splitting))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-subsampling**: Use when comparing cell type matching methods (e.g., TACTiCS vs SAMap) whose reference performances were computed on differently sized datasets, and you need equal-sized per-species subsets to control for dataset size effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dataset-subsampling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-deep-clustering**: Use when scRNA-seq count data have already been embedded by a pre-trained graph autoencoder and cell-level cluster labels are needed, with simultaneous learning of embedding and cluster assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-deep-clustering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when two or more single-cell groups (e.g., cells assigned to mtDNA variants/clones by MQuad+vireoSNP) need to be compared transcriptomically to report up-/down-regulated genes with adjusted p-value cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-differential-expression-analysis))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-differential-expression-inference**: Use when you have a raw (non-normalized) scRNA-seq count matrix, cell-level covariates such as a binary disease status (e.g. t2d_sim), age, and cellular detection rate, plus sample IDs, and need per-gene DE inference while controlling for within-sample correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-differential-expression-inference))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dimension-reduction**: Use when noisy scRNA-seq count matrices (with high dropout) need unsupervised dimension reduction to 2D for cell-type visualization, k-means clustering benchmarks, or lineage/pseudo-time recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dimension-reduction))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dimensionality-reduction-embedding**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., Tabula Muris FACS marrow counts) needs 2D embedding with t-SNE/UMAP and a statistically grounded, per-cell assessment of whether the visible structure is real rather than stochastic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dimensionality-reduction-embedding))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-downsampling**: Use when evaluating whether a cell-type perturbation statistic (e.g., nDEG from pseudobulk DE testing or scDist distance) remains stable under varying numbers of cells per cell type in scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-downsampling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-dropout-imputation**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix has extensive dropouts (40%-90% missing values) and downstream tasks such as cell clustering, t-SNE visualization, differential expression, or pseudotime/trajectory inference are degraded by zero inflation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-dropout-imputation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-embryo-trajectory-analysis**: Use when you have a merged, quantile-normalized single-cell expression matrix from a temporal biological process (e.g. mouse pre-implantation stages, oocyte to late blastocyst) and need pseudotime ordering that reflects developmental progression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-embryo-trajectory-analysis))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-emt-lineage-inference**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g., Smart-Seq2 SCC EMT) needs attractor decomposition into multiple stable/hybrid states and quantification of transition flux through intermediate cell states between epithelial and mesenchymal attractors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-emt-lineage-inference))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-entropy-modeling**: Use when you need to determine whether an scRNA-seq cluster is a pure cell population, e.g. assessing purity loss from batch effects such as aggregating control and IFN-beta stimulated PBMCs or cells from multiple unrelated individuals (Kang et al., GSE96583). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-entropy-modeling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-expression-binning-discretization**: Use when a quantitative, rank-trend-based low-dimensional gene representation is needed from normalized scRNA-seq data, i.e., when binning continuous expression values into discrete levels to group quasi-trend-preserved genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-expression-binning-discretization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-expression-scoring**: Use when you have a preprocessed scRNA-seq expression matrix with cell-type labels and need to compute, rank, or compare expression-based ligand-receptor interaction scores across CCC methods and prior-knowledge resources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-expression-scoring))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-feature-representation-comparison**: Use when benchmarking whether feature construction/extraction methods improve the accordance between engineered cell features and true cluster labels in scRNA-seq data, measured via silhouette values against original data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-feature-representation-comparison))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-gene-detection-rate-filtering**: Use when a raw single-cell read count matrix is highly zero-inflated (e.g. ~70% zeros) and must be filtered to well-detected genes prior to normalization, correlation estimation, or network inference with scLink. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-gene-detection-rate-filtering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-global-scaling-normalization**: Use when raw UMI count matrices from a droplet-based scRNA-seq experiment (e.g. 10x cellranger filtered_bc_matrix or gene-by-cell tables) need QC, normalization, scaling and variable-feature detection prior to PCA/clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-global-scaling-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-hidden-layer-representation-extraction**: Use when a trained DL model (e.g., RFCN on the Zheng sorted scRNA-seq dataset) exists and you need its last hidden layer outputs projected into a 2D UMAP space to inspect cell-population separation by known cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-hidden-layer-representation-extraction))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-imputation**: Use when a sparse single-cell RNA-seq count matrix (especially 10x data, genes x cells) must be imputed before feeding the scFEA/FLUXestimator neural-network flux estimation on the M171 or Glucose-Glutamine module networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-imputation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-imputation**: Use when a droplet-based scRNA-seq expression matrix has a large fraction of false zeros (dropout) and downstream analyses such as cell-cell distance or correlation computation are degraded by undetected transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-imputation--230bd1567f2bb1df))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-imputation**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix is highly sparse (&gt;80% or even 95% zeros) and true expression levels behind excess zeros must be recovered computationally, treating all zeros as missing values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-imputation--d04cabb81a33e74f))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-in-silico-data-simulation**: Use when a runtime or performance benchmark must be repeated on controlled synthetic scRNA-seq data with the same dimensions as an existing benchmark (e.g., dataset_sizes = [1000..250000] cells × 10000 genes), especially to isolate the effect of UMI-count sparsity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-in-silico-data-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-kinetic-model-fitting**: Use when raw UMI or non-UMI scRNA-seq count matrices must be converted into gene-level transcription kinetic parameter distributions (kon, koff, s) for simulation or bursting analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-kinetic-model-fitting))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-label-noise-injection**: Use when you must quantify sensitivity of marker gene selection or cell-type classifiers to mislabeled training cells in scRNA-seq data (e.g. .h5ad AnnData with a group_by annotation column), at controlled corruption percentages for k selected markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-label-noise-injection))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-library-normalization**: Decision support for choosing between RAW, TC, TMM, or FQ normalization before scRNA-seq dimensionality reduction (PCA, ZIFA, ZINB-WaVE), when the analysis goal is to quantify how normalization choice affects clustering quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-library-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-log-normalization**: Use when the input is a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (genes × cells) that must be scaled by cell library size and log-transformed before embedding, similarity search, or feature gene selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-log-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-marker-selection**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain many cell types/tissues with overlapping DE genes and you need one global marker set that discriminates every phenotype pair (e.g., cell-type/tissue combinations in IPF, mouse cortex, or human cell atlas datasets). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-marker-selection--cb6045d425fe121e))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-marker-selection**: Use when a scRNA-seq AnnData dataset needs a small panel of marker genes (e.g., k=50 or fewer than 10% of genes) that maximize cell type classification accuracy, as in spatial transcriptomics panel design or marker benchmarking studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-marker-selection--862b14f50f0352ce))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-minmax-rescaling**: Use when an scRNA-seq expression matrix is normalized but unscaled and will feed a non-negative-kernel autoencoder or neural-network feature-selection/dimensionality-reduction step that requires bounded, non-negative input values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-minmax-rescaling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-multi-dataset-integration**: Use when several independently generated scRNA-seq datasets (different labs, platforms, or batches) must be merged so cells group by biological cell type rather than by study of origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-multi-dataset-integration))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (general or sparse) shows sequencing-depth, capture-efficiency, dropout, or composition biases and distribution-free normalization is needed before DE or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization**: Use when immediately after loading raw single-cell gene expression count matrices (AnnData objects) and before identifying highly variable genes or performing dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization**: Use when a scRNA-Seq expression matrix shows strong within-cell-type heterogeneity driven by cell-cycle genes (e.g. cells cluster by cell-cycle stage instead of cell type) and you need a corrected matrix before clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization--6bed3281f4780147))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI count matrices must be normalized before association testing (DE, co-expression, or CRISPR screen) and unbiased, uniformly distributed null P values across gene expression levels are required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization--48ec6ba3236e6dd5))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count data (e.g. CD34_sc from MetacellAnalysisToolkit) must be normalized and embedded prior to building metacells or computing QC metrics like purity, compactness, or separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-scaling**: Use when a raw Seurat object of single-cell RNA-seq data must be prepared for Connectome's CreateConnectome — i.e. before ligand-receptor z-score calculation on the panc8, ifnb, or similar annotated scRNA-seq objects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-scaling--3da83145fe3181ad))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-scaling**: Use when a scRNA-seq Seurat object has already been through a coarse doublet-removal (overkill) step and you need normalized, scaled, PCA-embedded data to build k-means clusters for artificial doublet simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-normalization-and-scaling--3bf13f867d535d7a))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-object-merging**: Use when you have two or more independently pre-processed SingCellaR objects from biological replicates and need a single integrated object for downstream clustering, UMAP visualization, and cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-object-merging))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-perturbation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether a differential-state method (e.g., Augur) inflates false positives as patient-level variability (tau^2) grows, by simulating null scRNA-seq data with beta=0 and comparing against scDist's calibrated p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-perturbation-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing**: Use when a raw single-cell RNA-Seq expression matrix (genes x cells, FPKM/TPM) plus a per-cell sample table must be quality-filtered and normalized on a desktop/laptop before cell clustering and cell type identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing--bd8a6b5f58fdf76e))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing**: Use when an .h5ad Anndata from velocyto lacks imputed spliced/unspliced moments and must be prepared before running DeepCycle or scVelo-based cell cycle fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing--467d0c4e9c5582f2))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing**: Use when preparing single-cell count matrices (e.g., capital.dataset.setty19, velten17, paul15) for branched pseudotime trajectory inference or trajectory-tree alignment, where denoised, dimension-reduced expression is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing--1996ca5f90c30718))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices (e.g. cancer scRNA-seq from 10X or RDS) must be quality-filtered, normalized, and scaled before cell-type annotation, malignancy prediction, or feeding an XGBoost model in scCancer2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing--111eb75bfed55999))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing**: Use when an expression matrix (scRNA-seq counts or qPCR values) must be normalized before fitting Cyclum or before running baseline methods (PCA, Slalom, Cyclone, reCAT, Oscope, Cyclops) on periodic-process data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing--5125448714bb652f))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-and-clustering**: Use when raw single-cell expression files must be filtered, clustered into cell-type/subpopulation labels (e.g., microglia vs. DAM), and aggregated into per-sample counts feeding a compositional differential-abundance model such as scCODA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-and-clustering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-and-normalization**: Use when a dissociated scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq matrix (e.g. AD middle frontal neocortex snRNA-seq, ~122,606 nuclei, loaded via scanpy/anndata) must be normalized before a supervised spatial-domain or 2D-location prediction model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-and-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when raw UMI count matrices from scRNA-seq (e.g. Fluidigm C1, SMART-seq2, CEL-seq/2 or PBMC datasets) must be normalized, HVG-filtered, and standardized before clustering with DESC or similar deep-embedding methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-normalization--aecfd71096392024))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (genes x cells or cells x genes, e.g. .h5) needs filtering and log-normalization before running rare cell detection methods such as scCAD, CellSIUS, EDGE, FiRE, or SCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-normalization--5eb06e70fca387c1))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-seurat**: Use when a raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrix (e.g., GEO-supplied counts such as GSE141259 or the Hydra input_counts matrix) must be normalized, scaled, variable-feature-selected, and PCA-reduced before running scDEED or t-SNE/UMAP embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-preprocessing-seurat))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-priming-quantification**: Use when a snapshot or pseudotime-ordered scRNA-Seq dataset contains a multipotent (MPP/TAC) population plus two or more competing downstream lineages, and the goal is to detect lineage-primed states, rank candidate regulatory factors/pairs, and call individual primed cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-priming-quantification))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-processing**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices from GEO deposits (e.g. GSE145926 BALF or PBMC scRNA-seq) need QC filtering, LogNormalize normalization, PCA/UMAP embedding, graph-based cluster annotation, and severe-vs-mild differential expression testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-processing))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-pseudotime-inference**: Use when a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq matrix of a differentiating lineage needs pseudotime ordering or linear/branched trajectory detection, and development-related GO genes plus cell-type labels (e.g. llorens_labels) are available for benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-pseudotime-inference))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control**: Use when raw UMI count matrices (e.g., GSE123139 MARS-seq melanoma or GSE120861 Perturb-seq) are the input to Normalisr differential-expression or co-expression analyses and low-read/low-gene cells, sparsely detected genes, or outlier cells must be filtered first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-and-normalization**: Use when when you have a raw or minimally processed scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., a Seurat object loaded from GEO) and need to prepare it for pathway enrichment or coregulation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-and-normalization))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g., the EMT MCF10A/HuMEC mock vs TGF-β dataset) contains low-quality cells that must be removed before training a DNN or computing annotation-trainability scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices (.csv or .parquet) need QC summarization and low-quality cell/gene removal as a preprocessing step before normalization, HVG selection, and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering--db2ea0603d0f977f))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count data must be filtered (mitochondrial content, gene/cell detection thresholds) and normalized (TPM, log1p, HVG selection) prior to trajectory-preserving feature selection or trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering--6c93550d9d6d4133))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering**: Use when a raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrix from peripheral blood (e.g. GSE149689 PBMCs) must be QC-filtered and normalized in Seurat prior to Harmony integration, UMAP embedding, and cell-type-level differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-control-filtering--b259f2d25d6de17e))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-quality-filtering**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., AnnData .h5ad from HuBMAP datasets) must be cleaned of low-quality cells and sparsely/ubiquitously expressed genes prior to normalization, PCA, and Leiden clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-quality-filtering))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-query-subsampling**: Use when benchmarking how a single-cell annotation method's runtime scales with query dataset size, or when reproducing runtime scaling curves (e.g., Supplementary Figure S25A of scMAGIC) across competing RBC methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-query-subsampling))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking read-level scRNA-seq tools (UMI deduplication, aligners) requires synthetic reads (BAM/FASTQ) with ground-truth UMI count matrices, learned from a real BAM + GTF rather than untrained simulator defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-read-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-reference-subsetting**: Use when evaluating whether a reference-based cell type annotator can flag unseen cell types as 'Unknown': remove one cell type from the training reference and check predictions on held-out cells of that type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-reference-subsetting))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking or validating single-cell methods (clustering, multi-omics integration, GRN inference) requires synthetic scRNA-seq count matrices with known cell-type labels, replicates, experimental groups, and simulated gene-peak regulatory interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking how many DE genes are needed for a method (e.g. FiRE) to detect a rare cell subpopulation (~5% of cells) in simulated scRNA-seq data, or when building AUC-ROC sensitivity curves over DE-gene counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--c555309a301ca1bb))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when a new differential expression / differential pseudotime method must be evaluated for FDR control and power against gold-standard spike-in signals on multi-sample scRNA-seq data with pseudotime and a condition design matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--238b58c13a8206ff))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking pathway-analysis methods on single-cell data: you need controlled simulated two-population comparisons with a known implanted target pathway so detection sensitivity can be measured as a fraction of recovered scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--8b9248555ed36274))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when a benchmark with known ground-truth cell types and latent progression is needed to compare unsupervised feature selection or trajectory inference methods on simulated scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--cbbcadd16197d1ba))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when a new or existing rare-cell detection method needs quantitative benchmarking against competitors, and no real dataset with validated rare-cell ground-truth labels of controlled size and differential-expression level is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--bc0ad872e0f81e80))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking GRN inference algorithms on scRNA-seq data and reliable ground truth is needed, or when realistic in-silico scRNA-seq data with imposed TF-gene regulatory dynamics is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation--83e1402e07a01eee))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-simulation-scdesign2**: Use when you need a synthetic scRNA-seq dataset with known ground-truth trait-relevant cells (e.g., monocyte count) to quantify precision of per-cell scoring methods against GWAS-prioritized gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-simulation-scdesign2))
- **single-cell-rna-seq-umap-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when scRNA-seq data (e.g. log-cpm normalized AnnData from scanpy) needs a low-dimensional UMAP embedding for visualizing over-clustered Leiden labels and verifying Cytocipher cluster-merge results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-seq-umap-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-rna-spatial-data-integration**: Use when integrating scRNA-seq reference data with spatial transcriptomics (e.g., STARmap, MERFISH, 10x Visium) to obtain shared latent embeddings or deconvolve spatial spot cell-type proportions via an optimal transport plan. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-spatial-data-integration))
- **single-cell-rna-velocity-baseline-evaluation**: Use when you need to benchmark TF-based velocity estimation against splicing-based scVelo velocities, or test whether TF expression linearly explains RNA velocity on a scRNA-seq dataset with spliced/unspliced counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rna-velocity-baseline-evaluation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq**: This skill should be used when running single-cell RNA-seq analysis: reading 10x/AnnData matrices, QC filtering, normalization, HVG selection, PCA, clustering, UMAP/t-SNE and cell type annotation. 用于单细胞转录组（scRNA-seq）质控、降维、聚类与细胞注释全流程。 ([ssinder/bioinformatics-agent-skills](https://github.com/ssinder/bioinformatics-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-rnaseq) · ★76)
- **single-cell-rnaseq**: Single-cell RNA-seq analysis — QC, normalization, clustering, annotation, and trajectory inference. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/single-cell-rnaseq) · ★68 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-rnaseq**: Single-cell RNA-seq analysis (mirrors Claude Science's single-cell skill). scanpy/anndata pipeline QC-&gt;normalize-&gt;HVG-&gt;PCA-&gt;neighbors-&gt;Leiden-&gt;UMAP-&gt;markers-&gt;annotation, publication figures. Use for scRNA-seq, count matrices, cell clustering/annotation. Needs the sci-bio env. ([JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win](https://github.com/JWM0203/Claude-Science-for-win/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/single-cell-rnaseq) · ★37)
- **single-cell-rnaseq-age-binned-aggregation**: Use when you have an aggregated multi-tissue, multi-age single-cell RNA-seq AnnData object and need to algorithmically derive cell-type-specific signatures of an age-associated cellular state (e.g., senescence) from age-dynamic expression changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-age-binned-aggregation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-analysis**: 当处理单细胞 RNA-seq 数据（.h5ad/10X/CSV）需做质控、归一化、降维聚类、找 marker 基因与可视化时使用；用 Scanpy/AnnData 跑标准探索流程并产出 UMAP/聚类/细胞类型注释与处理后 .h5ad；不适用于深度学习概率模型（用 scvi-tools）、跨条件严谨差异表达（先 pseudobulk 再用 pydeseq2）；触发词：单细胞、scRNA-seq、scanpy、anndata、UMAP、聚类、marker 基因、细胞类型注释 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/single-cell-rnaseq-analysis) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **single-cell-rnaseq-autoencoder-clustering**: Use when clustering raw or normalized scRNA-seq count/expression matrices (rows = cells, columns = genes) with tens of thousands of genes or millions of cells, especially when the number of cell types is unknown and rare cell types may be present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-autoencoder-clustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-batch-effect-correction**: Use when clustering scRNA-seq data with strong batch effects across many batches (e.g., 30 samples), when batch information is unavailable, or when scaling to tens of thousands of cells and needing runtime/memory comparisons of correction methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-batch-effect-correction))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-annotation**: Use when an unsupervised clustering of scRNA-seq data (e.g., BUSseq with K chosen by BIC) has yielded clusters without labels and you must assign cell-type identities using marker-gene expression and reference datasets such as Haemopedia. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-annotation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-count-benchmarking**: Use when a spectral-clustering similarity matrix exists for a scRNA-seq dataset and you must determine or benchmark the number of clusters without using ground-truth labels for fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-count-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-purity-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters need a quantitative purity score (e.g. ROGUE &gt; 0.9 as pure) or when low-purity clusters (ROGUE &lt; 0.8) must be detected and re-clustered into refined subtypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-cluster-purity-quantification))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-clustering**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g. severe COVID-19 vs healthy bronchoalveolar/peripheral cells, smokers vs non-smokers airway epithelium atlas) needs Seurat graph-based clustering before DE gene extraction for E.PAGE enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-clustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-clustering-and-annotation**: Use when raw or unannotated scRNA-seq data (e.g., GEO-accessioned BALF, breast epithelial, or TNBC samples) must be quality-controlled, clustered, and assigned cell-type labels before downstream drug repurposing or per-cluster DEG computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-clustering-and-annotation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq dataset with known cell type labels is available and the goal is to test whether clustering the c-CSN-transformed degree matrix (CNDM) outperforms clustering the gene expression matrix (GEM), measured by ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-clustering-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-co-expression-inference**: Use when you have raw UMI count matrices from scRNA-seq (e.g., a Seurat object like blish_covid.seu.rds) and need unbiased cell-type-specific co-expression networks, or donor-level differential co-expression between conditions such as COVID-19 vs. healthy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-co-expression-inference))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw or processed scRNA-Seq expression matrices (e.g. hESC/progenitor populations like Chu et al.) must be prepared for network-based potency estimation with SCENT, requiring gene mapping to a signalling interaction network before computing per-cell signalling entropy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-count-matrix-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-count-simulation**: Use when benchmarking batch-effect correction or cell-type preservation methods and controlled synthetic scRNA-seq data are needed with known ground-truth cell types, batches, and effect magnitudes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-count-simulation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-data-loading-and-qc**: Use when starting from a 10x Chromium scRNA-seq matrix processed with Cell Ranger v6.0.1 and it must be prepared as input for CellOracle GRN inference/simulation or Capybara cell identity classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-data-loading-and-qc))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-data-preprocessing**: Use when a raw single-cell gene expression matrix needs filtering (min_cells=3, min_genes=200), total-count normalization, log1p and scaling before feeding into scAce or computing ARI/NMI clustering benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-data-preprocessing))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-dataset-acquisition**: Use when a benchmark or classification task requires downloading publicly available scRNA-seq datasets identified by GEO accession numbers (e.g., GSE104276, GSE108989) or supplement tables, and normalizing them per assay type before cell-type identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-dataset-acquisition))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-denoising-model-training**: Use when you need to demonstrate or verify that an scRNA-Seq denoising/imputation model's results (t-SNE patterns, ASW, ARI, MSE, zero-recovery) are insensitive to hyperparameter choices, benchmarked against DCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-denoising-model-training))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., GSE84133 Baron pancreas) and need a low-dimensional embedding that preserves inter-population centroid distances and developmental trajectory structure rather than only local neighbor structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-distance-estimation**: Use when you have a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix plus per-cell metadata (condition, patient, cluster/cell-type label) with replicates, and you need a statistically rigorous per-cell-type distance between condition means rather than a differential-abundance or classifier-based. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-distance-estimation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-doublet-detection**: Use when a scRNA-seq doublet analysis needs to boost Chord beyond its three built-in tools by appending scores from any external doublet detector to the saved chord() objects and re-running the ensemble. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-doublet-detection))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking computational performance (runtime, peak memory) of a scoring method across dataset sizes and you need controlled, identically sized single-cell expression matrices rather than testing only on the full million-cell atlas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-downsampling))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-imputation**: Use when a log2-transformed, library-size-normalized scRNA-seq count matrix (cells x top-1000 high-dispersion genes) contains dropout zeros that impair downstream clustering, expression recovery, or variance stabilization analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-imputation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-simulation**: Use when assessing robustness of bulk deconvolution (MuSiC) to zero-inflation in the single-cell reference: apply a dropout grid to a scRNA-seq ExpressionSet, re-estimate proportions with music_prop, and score against real proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-simulation--16067badfd2f77b0))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-simulation**: Use when benchmarking a scRNA-Seq imputation/denoising method against a known ground truth, or reproducing ablations (e.g., classifier-necessity test) that require paired dropout and dropout-free count matrices with known cell-group labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-dropout-simulation--541ee7f9bb0f0b42))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-enrichment-scoring**: Use when an AnnData object with log-cpm normalized counts and over-clustered (high-resolution) Leiden labels needs statistically grounded merging of non-significantly different cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-enrichment-scoring))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-feature-ranking**: Use when you have a preprocessed scRNA-seq expression matrix (e.g., the Mouse cortex MC/Zeisel et al. dataset) with phenotype labels and need a per-gene score to rank all features before selecting a discriminative marker set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-feature-ranking))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-feature-selection**: Use when clustering scRNA-seq data (e.g., 10X pbmc_1k_v2 counts) and you need an optimal, non-redundant feature gene set to improve cluster separation and DE-gene detection before PCA/clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-feature-selection))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-fusion-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating fusion gene detection performance on single-cell RNA-seq data with low sequencing depth, e.g. reproducing HVLD AUC values on K562 or multiple-myeloma scRNA-seq cohorts against competing models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-fusion-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-fusion-simulation**: Use when evaluating or comparing fusion detection methods on simulated single-cell RNA-seq data, or when quantifying how deep-learning/statistical filtering affects scFusion's precision and F-score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-fusion-simulation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-imputation-with-autoclass**: Use when raw scRNA-Seq count matrices (e.g., simulated Splatter data, Villani PBMC, Baron pancreatic islet) suffer from dropouts, noise, or batch effects and you need distribution-agnostic imputation/denoising with downstream clustering or batch-merging evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-imputation-with-autoclass))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-library-size-filtering**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. Drop-seq, thousands of cells) contains extreme-depth cells or very sparse genes that would distort downstream imputation or Gini-distribution comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-library-size-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-log2-preprocessing-and-gene-filtering**: Use when raw or low-level-normalized scRNA-seq count/TPM matrices feed an ensemble clustering or feature-selection pipeline and need normalization plus removal of low-level expressed genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-log2-preprocessing-and-gene-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-network-analysis**: Use when a scRNA-seq gene expression matrix must be transformed into per-cell direct gene-gene association networks, or when cell-specific hub genes/module structure (e.g., Gokce neural-differentiation hubs) need to be identified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-network-analysis))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-normalization**: Use when an annotated scRNA-seq count matrix must be converted to a comparable expression scale before gene selection or cell type classification, and the protocol (full-length vs UMI) dictates TPM+log1p or size-factor-10,000 normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-normalization))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-normalization-handling**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices must be converted to a comparable feature space (sf-log1p) before training or evaluating cell type classifiers such as scTab, TabNet, XGBoost, MLP, or linear baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-normalization-handling))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-null-gene-set-simulation**: Use when benchmarking whether gene set enrichment methods (iDEA, fGSEA, PAGE, GSEA, CAMERA) produce well-calibrated null p-values on zero-inflated scRNA-seq-like data, or when studying how gene set size / coverage rate affects calibration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-null-gene-set-simulation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a normalized and scaled gene-by-cell scRNA-seq matrix (e.g. PBMC3k with VST-derived variable features) needs PCA followed by t-SNE/UMAP 2D embeddings in a Seurat/SCALA workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-per-gene-descriptive-statistics**: Use when comparing synthetic scRNA-seq datasets against original count matrices to quantify per-gene distribution fidelity and to expose failures such as unreproduced bimodal gene distributions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-per-gene-descriptive-statistics))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-posterior-coverage-assessment**: Use when you have per-cell posterior samples of a circular (24 h) quantity such as circadian phase from a Bayesian single-cell method and need to verify that credible-interval uncertainty quantifications are well-calibrated against known true phases or sample-collection times. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-posterior-coverage-assessment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-power-analysis**: Use when planning an interindividual single cell RNA-seq differential expression or eQTL study and you need to choose sample size, cells per sample, and sequencing depth to maximize detection power under a fixed budget. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-power-analysis))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing**: Use when raw 10x scRNA-seq FASTQ files (with optional CITE-seq ADT and hashing data) must be converted to a filtered, clustered, cell-type-annotated expression matrix via a reproducible end-to-end workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing--f380976ee3c848e8))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing**: Use when droplet-based 10X Chromium scRNA-seq data (mouse aorta light-dark cycle, hepatocyte time course) must be loaded as anndata objects and subset to cell types for circadian phase inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing--53f127b954c701fa))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-and-clustering**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices (e.g., from liver transplant or SRA cohorts such as SRP313633) need normalization, clustering, and annotation into cell populations before downstream cell-cell communication or trajectory analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-and-clustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-filtering**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g., GEO datasets like GSE117872) must be cleaned prior to bulk-to-single-cell drug response prediction, and cells/genes failing QC thresholds must be removed without discarding heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-normalization**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq counts must be prepared as a log-normalized (genes x cells) matrix for full-dimensionality kernel methods (e.g. q-diffusion) that require no feature selection or dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-preprocessing-normalization))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-qc-filtering**: Use when raw scRNAseq FASTQ/output from a time-series (e.g., GSE223109 mouse dermis) must be QC-filtered, integrated across samples, clustered, and annotated into major cell types with marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-qc-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-quality-assessment**: Use when a 10X Genomics barcoded BAM file (with barcodes.tsv.gz from outs/filtered_feature_bc_matrix/) needs per-cell quality assessment based on gene body coverage skewness before downstream scRNA-seq analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-quality-assessment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-quality-control**: Use when raw scRNA-seq counts (Seurat object, csv/rds/h5 counts matrix, or 10X folder) enter the scWizard workflow and low-quality cells must be filtered prior to annotation of TME cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-quality-control))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-read-alignment**: Use when scRNA-Seq FASTQs must be aligned to hg38/mm10 for downstream TE expression analysis with SoloTE or scTE, where multi-mapping reads across repetitive elements must be retained rather than discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-read-alignment))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-reference-loading**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq reference (e.g. 10x Chromium, GSE173778) and a spatial transcriptomics dataset (e.g. 10x Visium slides) must be ingested as the starting point of a SpatialDDLS deconvolution workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-reference-loading))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-reference-pretraining**: Use when building a reference-based cell-type annotation model from multiple scRNA-seq datasets generated on different sequencing platforms and you need batch-effect-free, pre-trained weights for downstream label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-reference-pretraining))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-signal-filtering**: Use when scRNA-seq count data contain a nuisance or confounding signal (cell cycle, sex, spatial zonation, batch, healthy reference) that must be removed at the gene-expression level while preserving the biological attribute of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-signal-filtering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-similarity-learning**: Use when you have a noisy single-cell RNA-seq expression matrix (with dropouts and outliers) and need a cell-to-cell similarity/distance metric for de novo clustering, dimension reduction, or 2-D/3-D visualization of heterogeneous populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-similarity-learning))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-simulation**: Use when you need controlled synthetic scRNA-seq data with known cell types, patients, and true condition distances to benchmark differential-state methods or test scalability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-simulation--3748f19230409f07))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-simulation**: Use when benchmarking cell-cycle correction or latent-factor removal methods on synthetic scRNA-Seq data, or when testing how sparse an effect (fraction of control genes influenced) must be before a PCA-based detector like ccRemover misses it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-simulation--0e00da0a05140942))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-subclustering**: Use when a broad Seurat cluster is suspected to contain rare subpopulations (e.g. low median silhouette score, known rare cell types like CAF subtypes absent from original annotations) and standard Louvain clustering failed to resolve them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-subclustering))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-synthetic-data-generation**: Use when a small pilot scRNA-seq count matrix is available and larger synthetic data of the original study size is needed to plan downstream analyses such as clustering or cell-type abundance estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-synthetic-data-generation))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-topic-modeling**: Use when the input is a cell-by-gene scRNA-seq count matrix and the goal is to decompose expression into interpretable topics corresponding to latent biological functions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-topic-modeling))
- **single-cell-rnaseq-validation-splitting**: Use when tuning the scSHARP GCNModel (batch size, neighbor count k, EdgeConv layer count l, embedding size e) on scRNA-seq data with upstream consensus confident labels, and you need held-out cells to score candidate configurations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rnaseq-validation-splitting))
- **single-cell-rsec-clustering**: Use when single-cell count data must be partitioned into cell-type/identity clusters that will be supplied as labels to a trajectory-inference method such as Slingshot, especially after projecting cells into a ZINB-WaVE low-dimensional space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-rsec-clustering))
- **single-cell-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when a single-cell trajectory/dynamics method offers a pre-processing aggregation option for large datasets and you must quantify runtime speedup while verifying that downstream dynamical quantities (landscape, transition probabilities, path likelihoods) are preserved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/single-cell-runtime-benchmarking))
- **single-cell-runtime-memory-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime, memory usage, and clustering performance (ARI) of scRNA-seq integration/clustering methods across increasing dataset sizes, or quantifying device speed-up (CPU vs GPU) of a deep-learning single-cell model.
